## 2026-11-12 — Decision-time Evidence analytics (cohesive addition)
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-EVIDENCE-DECISION-TIME-FILTER-2026-11-12
- Authority: approved no-hindsight chain in MR-1.12, governing roadmap and Quick Workflow handoff. This is read-only analytics behavior, NOT a new mandatory observed-evidence field or an asserted trading edge.
- Existing Analytics Evidence type filter previously matched any candidate/trade observation, including evidence recorded after the trade's decision. That is valid as an *all-time* retrospective query but not as evidence known at the decision.
- Added **explicit optional Evidence timing** filter `ANY_RECORDED` (default; preserves existing analytics/report/export semantics) vs `AT_DECISION` (matches observations whose recorded timestamp is at/before the exact `candidate_decisions.occurred_at`). A missing/unparseable timestamp or missing decision time is conservatively excluded only from `AT_DECISION`. Timestamp comparison via SQLite `julianday` to account for offsets. Does not modify Evidence events, Decision events or calculations. UI displays the choice only after an Evidence type is selected.
- Changes to Rust `JournalFilterInput`, shared filtered journal query, TypeScript JournalFilter and React Analytics controls. Added analytics.evidenceTiming to complete All-Inputs *inventory*; NOT claimed customizable/accepted Field Manager support. Existing exports share the same filter object, so the selected temporal scope carries into report/CSV.
- Regression tests include earlier vs later candidate Evidence, later trade-linked Evidence, null decision time, unparseable evidence time, timezone offset comparison, `present=0`, and preservation of default any-time behavior.
- Source commits: `390a69bd3ab8e02b7d9aed8e90c50e39ae7c6c89`, `1104c952ed62655964020067892e7b2ff6a0e66d`, `8c03c9b18479c5d4077b962d47c9e3efc28d183b`, `e3be4cd5198f19752dcceede7cccefaa0839b996`, `a547e92e03510ede846adc7239cef29ecb4bdb34`.
- Independent GitHub workflow readback for exact code head `a547e92e03510ede846adc7239cef29ecb4bdb34`: Actions run **43077713903 COMPLETED SUCCESS**, Frontend SUCCESS, Rust SUCCESS. Earlier source run **43077333824 COMPLETED SUCCESS** also had both jobs successful. These are **CI-TESTED**, not installed Windows or accepted.
- Caveat: a record timestamp is a conservative *capture-time proxy*, not independent proof that the trader saw/understood evidence before acting. Historical Level Source labels still use current registry labels, although the source IDs are now revision-scoped. No fabricated causal/edge conclusions.
- Full All-Inputs Field Manager, Quick Workflow, historical Fill cost corrections, exact approved icon binary transfer, and native Windows acceptance remain OPEN/PARTIAL per controlling scope. Preserve all 12 Future Roadmap categories and all governing requirements. Work only on temporary public feature branch and draft PR #8. NO merge, private repo write, SQLite migration, original dataset alteration or installer deployment.

## 2026-11-12 — Quick Capture ambiguous-write protection
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-QUICK-DECISION-FILL-UNCERTAIN-WRITE-GATE-2026-11-12
- Scope and authority: cohesive, local-first Quick Workflow safety correction for already approved Decision -> Execution Fill capture. Preserves multiple independent decisions, repeated Zone entries, Review Pending, historical events and canonical SQLite. No change to persistence schema, cost calculations or original data. Mistaken upload `files.zip` explicitly ignored; it was not read, extracted or used.
- Identified defect: Decision capture catch block previously claimed `Decision not saved` on transport error despite server potentially committing before response loss. Fill capture warned but still allowed an immediate repeat click, potentially creating a duplicate fill.
- Added **fail-safe UI quarantine** for unconfirmed Decision and Fill writes: disable subsequent save for the SAME Zone or Trade immutable ID until a successful live history reload AND explicit manual retry acknowledgement. Show full ISO timestamps for up to 12 recent Zone decisions; refresh the saved execution timeline for Fill; do not fabricate outcome or silently auto-merge. Other Zones/trades remain usable. Reopening the same Zone in the current running process does not drop its uncertainty guard. The usual fast capture path adds no mandatory fields or steps.
- Shared source helper `app/src/writeOutcomeGuard.ts`, tests `app/src/writeOutcomeGuard.test.ts` cover owner scoping, absent owner, cross-owner history, missing check and successful verified history. Source commits `1660a1c2a37f0b48c5e709205868df38e2aabaa1`, `8f6907c3003a842ec95b0e368bc9d771527ace68`, `68e3614a04bd855d31578e459abab3659607b285`, `6f5530404c3476294871f0b378efb1e111997bd5`, `18cbf8709be60fd95c49021b071c955d61cafbb8`, `41e5932ba116e38b4b9509de57589585ac7a67bf`, `6e8bd3d4cb17e515bb5908146f649e085fecd660`, `725ade01c941196a55a43486bd04c9702fd26a24`.
- Independent CI read-back: exact feature source commit `725ade01c941196a55a43486bd04c9702fd26a24`, GitHub Actions **43082559763 COMPLETED SUCCESS**, frontend SUCCESS (tests/TypeScript/Vite) and Rust SUCCESS (cargo check/library tests). **CI-TESTED**, not WINDOWS-TESTED and not user ACCEPTED. Do not retest previous installed Windows PASS scenarios unnecessarily.
- **Residual limitation:** per-process UI quarantine is not cross-restart, server-authoritative idempotency, and a history refresh does not mathematically prove a missing write; the user must inspect the actual prior event before acknowledging. Any durable request-ID/idempotency protocol needs separate design/technical approval and migration safety assessment. Execution history display and Decision history text should be checked in next targeted native Windows acceptance.
- Governing roadmaps and all 12 Future Roadmap categories remain intact. Quick Workflow PARTIAL, Complete All-Inputs Field Manager PARTIAL, Audited Historical Fill Correction OPEN pending explicit approval. Exact approved icon binary not yet committed. Draft PR #8 UNMERGED, temporary public branch only. No modifications to private canonical repo, main, Windows installer, local SQLite or actual trading records.

## 2026-11-12 — Cohesive Evidence→Decision→Fill uncertain-write completion
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-QUICK-CAPTURE-UNCERTAIN-OBSERVATION-PACKAGE-2026-11-12
- Scope: approved local-first Quick Workflow, same persistent application, public isolated branch only. This material increment closes the in-process ambiguity treatment across all three capture actions: atomic multi-Evidence batch, Decision and Fill. **Not** completion of full Quick Workflow or All-Inputs Field Manager.
- Critical finding: existing `saveEvidence` catch claimed "Nothing saved in this batch" on exceptions, although an atomic SQLite batch may have committed before the response was lost. This could generate duplicate Evidence observations after blind retry, undermining decision/analytics provenance. Replaced with explicit UNKNOWN and a same-Zone retry quarantine, successful persisted history refresh requirement, and explicit manual acknowledgement after comparison. No extra steps on normal successful capture.
- On reopening an unresolved Zone, its attempted selection IDs, evidence classifications, direction, price and note are preserved in a session-memory, **Zone-keyed** snapshot so comparison remains possible even when switching Zones; no cross-Zone leaking. Recent Evidence list shows up to twenty original events with precise timestamps. Ordinary history, stored Evidence events and independent decisions/trades are unchanged. This is a client/runtime safety measure, not durable cross-restart or server idempotency.

## 2026-11-14 — Custom Field event-level verification and duplicate-write safety (source + CI)
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-CUSTOM-FIELD-EXACT-WRITE-VERIFICATION-2026-11-14
- User authorized continuing cohesive Settings / Field Manager and all originally approved roadmap requirements, including Personal Custom Fields; no separate DEMO for tiny changes and no broad UX/navigation redesign. Production and frozen DEMO 1.9.6-beta.1 baseline `c80ce859994127b7c961d0f3631a4094c4e7eda0` remain unchanged.
- **Verified source defect:** `OwnerCustomSelections` and `OwnerTypedCustomFields` previously reported successful event saving and updated their saved-local baseline immediately upon resolving an IPC write, without independently confirming that the new event ID and the selected typed value or historical option IDs/labels had become the current persisted owner-scoped event. If readback failed, SELECT displayed a generic saved notice and allowed another write. This could mislead users about persistence and permit repeated appended historical events after an uncertain result.
- **Implemented on temporary public feature branch only:** helper `app/src/customFieldWriteVerification.ts` compares the exact newly generated `event_id` plus typed value/type (including NUMBER 0, BOOLEAN false, DATE and TEXT) or exact option IDs with non-empty historic `label_at_selection` snapshots. App asks the existing local API for persisted current event *before* changing saved-local values, notifying the completion validator, or reporting success. An uncertain write/readback result now emits a distinct warning and disables retrying that same field until the session is reopened and persisted values inspected; avoids silent duplicate events. Does not modify canonical event schema, migration, Rust/SQLite, original immutable history or user data.
- **New regressions:** `customFieldWriteVerification.test.ts` verifies a current event is required rather than a matching stale prior event, and exact typed/option snapshots and non-finite/duplicate scenarios. Existing frontend suite and build passed. Exact code SHA `f79aed2175efdbb2cf6cc14ca43e9683b716fa1d` with CI-only run `43565396651` COMPLETED SUCCESS; npm ci, Vitest, TypeScript/Vite, SHA-locked approved icon resource test all PASS. NO new Windows installer or actual installed Windows test for this source.
- **Gate:** source IMPLEMENTED and frontend CI-TESTED; actual Rust native, Windows user interaction, end-to-end SQLite snapshots and prior-data migration effects NOT TESTED for this change. Full Roadmap #3 Full All-Inputs Field Manager and #4 Custom Fields & Options remain PARTIAL and OPEN, as do remaining approved categories and Master Requirements. Preserve prior user Windows PASS evidence for Reset/Required/Label baseline; no repeat without changed-path justification.
- **Next coherent development:** continue requirement-aware custom option/field management, stage-specific Quick vs Detailed and historical evidence, then one integrated DEMO at appropriate acceptance gate. No merge, no private repo changes or live DB writes without user's independent approval.

## 2026-11-14 — Active custom field/option label collision guard (CI-only)
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-CUSTOM-LABEL-ACTIVE-COLLISION-GUARD-2026-11-14
- **User instruction:** continue completing cohesive Settings / All-Inputs Field Manager functionality, maintain a large coherent acceptance batch, no micro-installer or large GitHub artifact churn; no private repo or production DB work.
- **Issue and scope:** existing Custom Fields administrative editor allowed active definitions in the same capture stage, or active options in one choice field, to share effectively identical names differing only by case/whitespace. This creates ambiguous prompts/choices and undermines downstream categorical analysis, despite stable underlying IDs. Inactive historical definitions/options must be retained, and same labels on different stages/fields may be legitimate.
- **Implementation:** `app/src/customFieldLabelSafety.ts` uses NFKC Unicode normalization, whitespace collapse and case folding to detect active same-stage / same-field collisions, excluding the unchanged current ID and ignoring inactive historical entries. `App.tsx` checks the current persisted definition/option list before creating, renaming or reactivating a field/option. Validation errors are displayed without treating them as an uncertain DB write or locking all subsequent edits; genuinely uncertain writes remain blocked until fresh readback. No migrations or changes to existing event IDs, historical option label snapshots or old definitions.
- **Tests:** `app/src/customFieldLabelSafety.test.ts` covers case/whitespace collisions, own-ID rename, inactive historical labels and blank names. CI-only workflow now triggers on both helper and test changes, with no installer artifact. GitHub Actions `43572448459` COMPLETED SUCCESS for source SHA `f9e4bc9aae746b929840c3e380cc137298c50859` (npm tests, TypeScript/Vite build and icon source verification). The user-installed Windows workflow is not yet tested for this code, and the broad Field Manager remains PARTIAL.
- **Authority:** user-approved separate isolated public development branch only, accepted source baseline `c80ce859994127b7c961d0f3631a4094c4e7eda0` unchanged, private canonical repository and main untouched, no actual personal journal/database writes. User's previously accepted Windows evidence remains valid only for unchanged paths. All 12 approved roadmap categories are retained; no scope changes or merges.
- **Open next action:** verify actual Quick/Detailed conditional field navigation and stage focus, custom option persistence and migration/backups on isolated Windows data, and trace all remaining Field Manager inputs/ordering. ## 2026-11-14 — Critical admin recovery deadlock and custom choice write guard
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-CUSTOM-ADMIN-RECOVERY-AND-CHOICE-SAFETY-2026-11-14
- **User's new reporting rule:** classify verified defects by severity. Report high-impact issues immediately and consolidate minor fixes instead of repetitive notices. Continue complete cohesive package; accuracy, safety and full approved requirements take priority over speed; no new Windows installer for small fixes.
- **High-impact confirmed frontend defect:** `CustomFieldDiscovery` correctly locks edit/reorder/cleanup actions after an uncertain database write, but its only recovery action `Refresh custom fields` was incorrectly `disabled={busy||uncertainAdmin}`. This made recovery impossible in the same Settings screen precisely when the warning instructed the user to Refresh. It did not prove or imply actual record loss. Fixed to allow Refresh whenever no other operation is busy, including uncertain-write state, while still disallowing new writes. Pure `customAdminRecovery.ts` and tests encode distinct busy and uncertain edit/recovery policy; `App.tsx` uses it.
- **Related data-integrity bug:** `OwnerCustomSelections` allowed saving a malformed existing `SINGLE_SELECT` selection with multiple distinct active IDs as a new event, despite the stage Required validator correctly rejecting more than one saved ID. Also Save could remain enabled for an empty choice. Added `customSelectionDraftSafety.ts` shared by Save handler and Save enabled-state. It requires 1 active option for SINGLE_SELECT, 1–100 distinct active options for MULTI_SELECT, and rejects duplicate/retired IDs. Post-write readback additionally enforces typed selection cardinality/historical snapshot validity before reporting success. Tests cover malformed saved legacy selections without changing old records. The hard-coded user-visible remedy is selecting one valid active option; old immutable history remains preserved.
- **Source and CI:** exact tested code SHA `f9439b747986b40408cceb8cceef1407053fe181`; GitHub Actions installer-free run `43574505408` COMPLETED SUCCESS: npm ci, Vitest, TS/Vite build, pinned icon check. Workflow paths include new helpers/tests. This is frontend source/CI proof only; no Rust-native SQLite test and no actual Windows user acceptance yet.
- **No migration / private merge:** development only on temporary public `feature/ptj-settings-from-accepted-demo-2026-11-14`; locked accepted DEMO baseline source `c80ce859994127b7c961d0f3631a4094c4e7eda0`, private canonical repo, main and original user DB untouched. No installer artifact generated. Keep all twelve roadmap families and full Field Manager/Custom Fields category #3/#4 PARTIAL until actual acceptance and end-to-end stage/Quick/Detailed/DB verification.
- **Next:** aggregate remaining risk-driven focused fixes, then one isolated Windows DEMO for package closure covering failure/Refresh recovery, malformed single-select, Required/Conditional focus, typed/option snapshots, and backup safety. Do not require user to recreate a dangerous database failure in a live journal; test with controlled injected failure or isolated DEMO data.

## 2026-11-14 — Cohesive custom admin recovery, valid choice event and byte-safe typed input gate
Checkpoint ID: PTJ-CUSTOM-RECOVERY-SELECTION-TEXT-NATIVE-CONTRACT-2026-11-14
- **User instructions:** proceed and report high-severity defects individually; aggregate multiple small issues before user-visible reports. Accuracy/data integrity/approved requirements prioritized over speed. Do not generate small Windows installers/Artifacts.
- **High severity frontend recovery defect identified and fixed:** after an administrative field-definition/option write produced an unverified result, CustomFieldDiscovery intentionally locks all further edits to prevent double writes. However its only recovery control, `Refresh custom fields`, was also disabled by `uncertainAdmin`, trapping users permanently in the warning state within the open Settings. Changed Refresh disabled condition to depend on `busy` only, preserving write lock for uncertain state. Pure `customAdminRecovery.ts` and `customAdminRecovery.test.ts` encode and test recovery vs editing permissions. No silent retry, forced reset, database rewrite, or exception to historical integrity.
- **Related selection integrity defect fixed:** existing invalid SINGLE_SELECT historical event could carry multiple active option IDs, and editor previously allowed re-appending that same malformed selection as a new event; UI Save could also remain enabled for an empty selection. `customSelectionDraftSafety.ts` provides a shared selection preflight for editor button and handler: SINGLE_SELECT exactly one active option; MULTI_SELECT 1-100 unique active options. Readback additionally requires selection event identity and correct cardinality/historical labels. Tests in `customSelectionDraftSafety.test.ts` cover malformed single, repeated IDs, inactive options and empty selections. No original historical event deletion/modification.
- **Minor related input validation:** native Rust `record_typed_value` rejects TEXT values beyond 4,000 UTF-8 bytes and control characters. Existing frontend TEXT field had no length cap or native-contract preflight, risking preventable backend rejections being shown as uncertain persistence failures. Added UI maxLength and `isValidCustomTextDraft` using TextEncoder UTF-8 length, non-empty trimmed content and forbidden C0/DEL codepoint check; regression tests check 4000/4001 ASCII and 2000/2001 Unicode cases. Source and tests isolated from native DB. Other field types unchanged.
- **Latest CI evidence:** source SHA `87f46940dd593a1416469cbbfd129e8534b323aa`, GitHub Actions `43575201646` COMPLETED SUCCESS, frontend Vitest, TypeScript/Vite and locked selected icon checks. Previous `43574505408` passed recovery/choice subset; no installer artifact created. This is NOT a native Rust/SQLite, Windows installed DEMO or user-acceptance PASS.
- **Requirements/status:** All-Inputs Field Manager (#3) and Custom Fields & Options (#4) remain PARTIAL. All 12 roadmap families and cumulative MR requirements remain in scope; isolated Windows DB history/backup and cross-stage Quick/Detailed/conditional focus gates still pending. Existing accepted source baseline `c80ce859994127b7c961d0f3631a4094c4e7eda0`, private repo, original user DB and existing installer untouched, no merges or migrations.
- **Next:** focused source audit for other high-severity data/validation inconsistencies, one combined native isolated DB / Windows acceptance gate after coherent package is ready; no micro-demo or arbitrary frontend CI loops.

## PTJ-SAVED-CUSTOM-VALUE-INTEGRITY-2026-11-15
**Source scope:** User-authorized cohesive Field Manager / Custom Fields package; approved baseline DEMO `1.9.6-beta.1`, SHA `c80ce859994127b7c961d0f3631a4094c4e7eda0` remains immutable. **Material source change:** A saved custom TEXT value must now meet the existing UI/native text validity boundaries (trimmed nonempty, <=4000 UTF-8 bytes, no ASCII control characters) before it can count as satisfying a currently Required field. Saved BOOLEAN must be an actual boolean, not merely a nonnull value. Read-back of an immutable SINGLE/MULTI_SELECT event must have valid unique IDs, nonblank historical labels and consecutive zero-based ordinals in native SQLite ORDER BY ordinal; malformed legacy events are reported incomplete rather than silently deemed verified. A newly written selection is not labelled 'saved and verified' until its exact event ID, option IDs, historical labels and ordered ordinal snapshots read back as expected. This is a frontend-only non-destructive validation/verification guard; historical records/events/snapshots remain unchanged, and current saved valid values stay valid.

**Regression files:** `app/src/customFieldRequired.test.ts` and `app/src/customFieldWriteVerification.test.ts` exercise malformed text/boolean and damaged selection ordinals, including preserved historical option labels.

No installer, PR merge, schema migration, DB operation, private repository change or overwrite.

### G4 placement feasibility update (read-only source audit)
- Examined actual `app/src-tauri/src/commands.rs` and `app/src/services/localApi.ts`: field visibility and placement preferences currently live as JSON values under `app_settings` keys `field_visibility_preferences_v1` and `field_placement_preferences_v1`; each save command already validates at most 200 entries, max 128-byte field key, and supported values. Native custom-field definitions still have NO separate Quick/Detailed placement/visibility.
- **Proposed, NOT approved/implemented:** isolate dynamic Custom Field visibility/placement in a new `app_settings` JSON preference key with explicit owner-stage and stable field-ID validation, preserve default visibility and always display Required, and retain historical values. This *may avoid any SQLite table migration*, but entails a new verified persistence contract/command, Field Manager/UI binding, Windows backup/restore compatibility check and safe fallbacks on malformed/stale settings. Reusing static keys without namespace is unsafe due to possible collisions; field IDs can reach 128 chars, so simple prefixing needs careful limits or a separate map.
- No technical authorization is inferred from this feasibility study. Do not write production DB, migrate data or implement a new persistence contract without applicable explicit approval. Exact design/status is PROPOSED; Group 4 remains PARTIAL.

7. Finish PLAN→DECISION→TRADE→REVIEW test workflow, with coherent usable Quick/Detailed experience. Record each PASS/FAIL, screenshots/logs, app installer path/version & file hash locally; no automatic acceptance or main merge.

## PTJ-PACKAGE-C-AUDITED-CORRECTION-DESIGN-GATE-2026-11-15
**Status: DESIGN PROPOSED / NOT APPROVED FOR MIGRATION OR WRITE IMPLEMENTATION. No runtime behavior changed.**
**Continuity source:** Previous user Windows approval `PTJ-PACKAGE-B-USER-WINDOWS-ACCEPTANCE-2026-11-15`, checkpoint HEAD `fb9c15c93b5489024160bf252cfbd64afb891477`. Package B isolated Windows DEMO accepted by user; #6 and #7 whole-clause coverage still PARTIAL. Accepted icon appearance must not be revisited absent a new defect.

### 1. Authority and exact governing requirements
- Master Product Roadmap R-2026.10 v1.0 **GATE 6 / v1.9F-2**: event-style correction of mistaken **recording** with old/new/reason/time/operator/field/entity, immutable original, no conflation with genuine plan/market revision; effective-value view in risk, fills, sources, timestamps, relations, grouping, attachments, exports, analytics and backup/restore; rollback of faulty migration; after explicit design review and user authorization.
- Cohesive Delivery Policy **Package C #8–9**: correction migration is a separate gate; analytics must use canonical effective values and explicit sample sizes, without invented P&L or historical evidence. Global Continuity Policy v1.2, MR-1.0..1.12 + approved MR-1.13..1.19 and Quick Workflow addendum all remain in force. New corrections must not weaken completed-review locks or field-manager/history snapshots.
- This record is a **decision-ready design**, NOT authorization to migrate, merge, expose production data, or implement correction writes.

### 2. Verified present implementation / grounded gaps
- App stack: React/TypeScript/Tauri/Rust/SQLite local-first. `app/src-tauri/src/db.rs` current schema **v9**, migrations 001..009, `open_database` auto-calls migrate; therefore shipping an unapproved migration would expose any opened app data. Do NOT do so.
- Immutable SQLite triggers in `migrations/001_initial.sql` for plan/candidate revisions and candidate decisions; `003_execution_core.sql` blocks UPDATE/DELETE for execution fills and risk snapshots. `004_post_trade_review.sql` protects completed Trade Reviews; `005_session_review_rules.sql` protects completed Session Reviews and locked rule checks. No pre-existing approved correction event table or audited overlay was located in reviewed source. Status #8 **NOT IMPLEMENTED**.
- `repository.rs` `trade_execution_ledger` derives moving-average cost basis and gross P&L from `execution_fills` ordered by stored event timestamp/rowid; `trade_metrics` derives net when **all** commissions/fees known and R only with valid initial risk. `list_journal_trades` / `get_analytics_summary`, `exports.rs`, `backup.rs`, `restore.rs` depend on persisted canonical data. Future correction must reconcile these paths rather than patch only the UI.
- Two identities: trading **event corrected** (data-entry transcription mistake) vs new legitimate event/revision (new market information, later stop move/plan change). No historical event ever physically updated/erased.

### 3. Proposed bounded user workflow (subject to explicit approval)
A) From the existing read-only Trade/Decision/Session history, click **Correct recorded data** on a supported field; display original immutable value + current effective value + entity/date/source.
B) Select corrected value, enter an **obligatory reason** and show a preview: affected trade/Fill/risk/R/net/report identity, invalid quantity/tick/chronology warnings, downstream grouped records. No auto-fix of other records and no hidden default acknowledgment.
C) Confirmation writes one append-only correction event only if the expected prior effective version still matches (optimistic concurrency / stale-write rejection). Return audit receipt (correction id, event time, operator USER, affected path and old/new).
D) Display **Recorded original | Effective now | Correction history** clearly. The corrected value never masquerades as an original observed fact. A new correcting correction supersedes an earlier correction by appending another event, not deleting or mutating history. A normal post-Trade Review remains process evaluation, not a recording correction.
E) Quick mode: small entry-point only in saved-history views; detailed audit explanation on demand, no mandatory forms during live execution.

### 4. Proposed persistence architecture (NOT an approved schema)
- New append-only `correction_events` (exact migration name TBD) with stable `correction_id`, target `entity_kind/entity_id/field_path`, serialized typed before/after plus format-version/type, immutable original reference, `created_at_utc`, `operator_id or USER`, mandatory reason, `supersedes_correction_id`, optimistic `expected_revision`, source/provenance and optional grouping/transaction id.
- Whitelisted correctable fields with typed validators and ownership checks. **No arbitrary user-supplied SQL path/field name, raw SQL, entity rewrite or JSON injection into query strings**. Cross-reference corrections to Session/Zone/Decision/Trade/Fill/Review owners require specific constraint checks; not generic scalar edits. Read-only originals remain queryable by ID and native audit view.
- One validated effective-value resolver (original plus active non-superseded correction chain) in Rust, reused by trade detail, ledger, risk snapshots, queue, analytics, exports and backup/restore. No duplication of business rules in React. Same effective-value snapshot across transaction; ambiguity/conflicting corrections fail closed.
- Avoid `NULL=0`: unknown costs/risk stay unknown, including corrections. Correcting time can change ledger order; recalculate entire affected trade chronologically in the preview, prohibit impossible or duplicate/over-sized exits. Tick, multiplier and instrument snapshot provenance must remain exact.
- Never silently change initial risk denominator when altering entry/stop: require a separately represented, explicitly reviewed correction to the *recorded* initial risk snapshot as applicable; report incomplete/inconsistent linked corrections as **unresolved**, not as trustworthy net R. Genuine subsequent Stop changes remain new risk snapshot events.
- Preserve original link IDs and recorded session/decision/trade associations. **Relation reassignment**, historical timestamp changes, source/provenance changes, attachment ownership, and completed-review alterations are **HIGH-RISK**; require individually specified semantics, negative tests and approval before inclusion in writable v1. Do not silently exclude these from final GATE 6 closure: keep as explicit OPEN subcoverage.
- Backup and restore must retain both original and correction ledger, validate referential integrity/audit chain and use isolated staging before any replace-live operation. On migration failure the existing DB/schema/records must be byte-logically preserved (as applicable to WAL) and rollback independently tested; never assert hashes alone prove safe backup.

### 5. Exact design-review/approval gates and first implementation slice
**C-Design gate (current):** this design record and full field/consumer impact inventory reviewed by user. No code/migration action approved yet.
**C1 (requires explicit user design + isolated migration authorization):** append-only scalar correction events for narrowly whitelisted *input mistakes* in Fill price, Fill quantity, commission, fees (and optionally text note only after review), read-only before/after/audit UI, preview and fail-closed validations. Fully supported ES/GC multi-Fill, costs, partial quantities and unknown-amount scenarios. Original tables immutable, dedicated migration + rollback on brand-new fixture and copy of representative isolated test data; no production journal opened. Include correction semantics in trade detail, derived net/R, analytics, CSV/HTML and backup-staging validation before calling C1 feature complete.
**C2 (requires subsequent explicitly reviewed semantics):** correction of event timestamps, Fill source/provenance, risk snapshots, Session/Zone/Decision/Trade relations, attachment ownership and historical completed review/other immutable metadata. Each has potential evidentiary/legal/logical impact. GATE 6 remains PARTIAL until all approved required cases are reconciled.
**C3 (#9 approved outcome learning; can be developed read-only separately):** report/sample-size and weekly-learning gaps on uncorrected/effective data, constrained by corrected dataset consistency when correction support exists.

### 6. Test and acceptance matrix (design; no PASS claim)
- Existing accepted Package B Windows ES/GC and icon results reused, not repeated unless correction affects calculations/UI.
- Native SQLite v10 hypothetical migration only AFTER approval: from v9, idempotence, foreign-key checks, transactional rollback on injected failure, correct chain/superseding events, immutable-original database triggers, staged restore and ZIP contents.
- Execution: ES/GC long/short + partial exit and re-entry, unknown vs known costs and risk denominator, correction to recorded fill price/qty, negative quantities/ticks, impossible event chronology, stale optimistic revision, wrong owner, duplicate correction, edit after completed Review, source/provenance mismatch.
- Analytics: effective trade P&L/R matches per-trade detail; filter/group/weekly sample count and net costs reaggregate; NO_TRADE/MISSED remain unexecuted, original fill still auditable. HTML and CSV export show effective value explicitly and retain correction trail/provenance as approved.
- UX: original/effective display and mandatory reason, explicit preview and confirmation, accessibility/Quick-to-Detailed consistency, cancelled correction unchanged, reread after app restart.
- Windows isolated DEMO: one meaningful combined Package C acceptance scenario only after source/migration and backup gates PASS; no installer for documentation-only design. Real user DB remains untouched pending separate authorization.

### 7. Open decisions / required user authorization
1. Approve or amend this proposed model: non-destructive append-only correction events with effective-value overlays, reason/preview/audit; distinct from market/plan revisions.
2. Approve implementing C1 specifically **on an isolated development branch and disposable SQLite test data**, including a NEW versioned migration there but **no change to original/private repository or actual user database**. Until authorization, no migration / correction write code.
3. For C2 high-risk relation/source/time/attachment correction semantics: remain OPEN and NOT ASSUMED, to be decided before their implementation.
**Current status:** #8 PROPOSED DESIGN / APPROVED PRODUCT REQUIREMENT / NOT IMPLEMENTED. #9 existing PARTIAL. Other twelve categories unchanged. #6/#7 Windows user acceptance reused; full MR closure still PARTIAL.
**Verified starting public branch/head at inspection:** `feature/ptj-settings-from-accepted-demo-2026-11-14` @ `fb9c15c93b5489024160bf252cfbd64afb891477`. Do not infer a code test or workflow run from this docs-only checkpoint.

Matching settings are shown in both Settings and Safety: native Windows directory picker, optional manual full folder path (e.g. D: drive; existing accessible directory required), clear Saved destination display, explicit Save, Use default, Create backup now; preference saved and independently read back before stating success. Clearly flags unknown entry criteria, contrary evidence, market regime, invalidation and Setup version; does NOT save a Setup, create entry triggers or claim validated edge. Jest/Vitest tests cover provenance and null R. The versioned/approved Setup Playbook schema, user-defined setup/version history, observations and persisted playbook workflow remain UNIMPLEMENTED / proposed pending independent scope/design decisions.
**Group #11 first **READ-ONLY** deliverable:** `app/src-tauri/src/import_readiness.rs` and `NinjaTraderImportReadinessPanel.tsx` provide manual selection of a local .csv (<=10 MB); bounded UTF-8, quote-aware, delimiter-validated header inspection and display of exact column names, never a trade import. The service reads only the first header record (max 32 KiB, not trade rows), requires unique nonempty headers, fails closed on ambiguous or malformed data. Returns `ready_for_import=false`, `rows_imported=0` and explicit required evidence (actual export type/field semantics, ES/GC contract/ticks, local/UTC DST, Fill & fee mapping, duplicate avoidance, independent dry run). No SQLite write, automatic Fill/Trade mapping, trading signal, NinjaTrader integration, new shared data silo or prior decision rewriting. ## PTJ-PACKAGE-A-SETUP-IMMUTABLE-SELECT-AND-UNCERTAIN-WRITE-GUARD-2026-11-17
Date 2026-11-17 Australia/Sydney. Approved ongoing Package A, TEMPORARY PUBLIC repo only. Current source HEAD before checkpoint: `131d77a072bf7584cb445d4c79b16062378584f5`; branch `feature/ptj-group10-cohesive-ux-fixes-2026-11-17`. Compare from prior checkpoint `3d152d9f8c51b999b4ef4f6a14fcbd7586b0aa44` verified ahead=8, behind=0; only five Setup-editor/helper/test source files changed, no DB schema, migrations or other product modules altered.

SOURCE IMPLEMENTED, REMOTE READBACK VERIFIED; ALL NEW TEST EXECUTION STILL PENDING:
1. `app/src/setupDraftLifecycle.ts` centralizes blank draft and immutable-version snapshot copying. Switching the saved-version selector to empty or unknown now yields `setup_id:null`, resets the editor draft, and clears selected version; switching to a real version preserves intended original `setup_id` for a deliberate new immutable revision. Prior bug: blank selector cleared UI selection but retained old setup_id in draft, which could misattribute an apparent independent Setup.
2. `SetupPlaybookWorkspace.tsx` now uses shared switch lifecycle and clears temporary historical trade seed on close/new/switch. A draft's evidence-priority list is copied by value rather than mutating the saved object. `app/src/setupDraftLifecycle.test.ts` added tests for blank/missing ID, valid revision and immutability.
3. Setup save now tracks whether a write command was attempted. A failed invocation or failed post-write readback is UNKNOWN, not automatically failed. It enters `uncertainSave`, blocks repeated Save and competing Setup transitions/deactivation/attribution until the outcome is reconciled or explicitly manually reviewed. `verifyUncertainSave` fetches latest versions and accepts SUCCESS only for the exact returned immutable `version_id`, never by name or related setup_id; if no returned ID or no exact match, keeps the write blocked. Explicit manual unlock is labelled as confirmation only after verifying no new version was saved; cannot falsely auto-declare that verification. Recovery logic in `app/src/setupSaveReadback.ts`, tests in `app/src/setupSaveReadback.test.ts`.
4. An unverified POST-WRITE case is not a reason to double-submit a newly independent Setup (`setup_id:null`); original data and history remain untouched. No backend behavior altered and no duplicate SQLite transactions introduced by this delta.

Evidence limits: GitHub connector exact file readback + compare only; Vitest, TypeScript/Vite, Rust, CI, installer, installed Windows and user acceptance NOT YET RUN for this new batch. Prior Windows user-reported Group10 PASS still preserved for pre-existing baseline only. This is a source gate, NOT an installed functional acceptance. No private repository writes, PR merge, schema migrations, live DB access, backup modification, installer or DEMO creation. Keep all 12 roadmap categories and remaining full All-Inputs Field Manager, Custom Fields and Audited Data Correction OPEN. ## PTJ-PACKAGE-A-REQUIRED-CUSTOM-FIELDS-PREF-READ-FAIL-SAFE-2026-11-18
Date: 2026-11-18 Australia/Sydney. APPROVED Package A, public-only branch `feature/ptj-group10-cohesive-ux-fixes-2026-11-17`. **Concrete Package A correctness gap:** On a failed/unavailable Custom Field presentation-preferences read, parent sets `customPresentationReady=false`. The two actual custom capture editors (select and typed TEXT/NUMBER/DATE/BOOLEAN) previously returned null even for required active custom definitions. Both OwnerCustomSelections and OwnerTypedCustomFields now use this policy and render Required entries in a visible expanded disclosure even when preference read failed; show an explicit warning that optional fields are withheld until preferences reload. `customFieldPresentation.test.ts` adds regression across all seven canonical owner stages, hidden optional and inactive cases, and restoration of saved optional Quick/Detailed preference after successful read.

**Verification:** GitHub remote readback of all three edited files, exact new helper and both form bindings, 7-stage test presence; all static checks true. No source checkout available in this environment (github.com DNS unresolved), and no installed Rust toolchain; Vitest/TypeScript/Vite/Rust/CI/Windows tests and user acceptance for this delta are UNKNOWN/NOT RUN. Do not call this gate PASSED or Package A complete. Preserve all 12 roadmap categories and Master Requirements; full Field Manager and Custom Fields remain PARTIAL. ## PTJ-PACKAGE-A-TYPED-NUMBER-PREWRITE-AND-CI-2026-11-20
- **Date:** 2026-11-20 Australia/Sydney. **Material Package A additive checkpoint.** Work only on PUBLIC `feature/ptj-package-a-cohesive-validation-repair-2026-11-18`, PR #14 still DRAFT, no merge/private changes. - **Concrete Custom Field issue:** `app/src/customTypedDraftSafety.ts` used `Number(raw)` and `Number.isFinite` alone to approve NUMBER drafts. JavaScript accepts `0x10`, `0b101`, `0o17` as decimal-converted numbers (16,5,15 respectively) rather than trader-entered decimal. Since `OwnerTypedCustomFields.save` serializes `Number(raw)` in an immutable numeric value event, source-level validation should not silently reinterpret a draft. This is a client prewrite safety/clarity issue, no historical data repair or schema effect.
- **Source changes:** `a689a9fcd35a7c28225855c38247d54eeb70dfd4` constrains custom NUMBER to optional signed decimal or scientific notation and then checks finite conversion; preserves zero, negatives and exponents. `6178658e9c2d27b8c6e46d77d98c0c928c90796a` adds positive/negative Vitest cases including prefixed-base, malformed and nonfinite numbers. `OwnerTypedCustomFields` already invokes the validator before `localApi.recordCustomFieldTypedValue`; no new database event on invalid input.
- **Exact-head CI gate:** GitHub Actions run `44538913707` on code SHA `6178658e9c2d27b8c6e46d77d98c0c928c90796a`: Frontend SUCCESS was observed, Rust in progress as of this checkpoint. Full run remains **PENDING** until independently reread. Intermediate CI runs may be cancelled by concurrency; only exact-head final conclusion counts. Do not present pending as PASS.
- **Direct field wiring audit:** actual `OwnerTypedCustomFields` reads the canonical owner-stage and typed current value on owner change, validates its draft before append, verifies the returned persisted event, blocks ambiguous retry, and keeps original history. `OwnerCustomSelections` separately handles selection snapshots. The active Field Manager visibility/placement registry contains 39 controls of 102 inventoried; unconnected candidate flags include intentionally locked Setup and conditional psychology controls, so their forced activation would violate approved semantics. Still need real end-to-end multi-owner stage/form audit, not just pure inventory tests.

## PTJ-PACKAGE-A-MULTI-ZONE-DECISION-EVIDENCE-GATE-2026-11-20
- **Date:** 2026-11-20 Australia/Sydney. **Material cohesive Package A source+regression checkpoint**; user directs continuation exclusively on temporary PUBLIC repository, on branch `feature/ptj-package-a-cohesive-validation-repair-2026-11-18`; draft PR #14 unmerged. - **Identified real multi-Zone Quick workflow gap:** Zone card `+ Evidence` directly calls `openEvidence(c)` (rather than notebook navigation). When user is on the DECISION page with an unsaved/uncertain Decision editor for any Zone, this direct path could set the Evidence owner/Quick Zone without consulting the same app-visible navigation gate as notebook tabs. The user would appear in another workflow stage with the unfinished Decision context not explicitly resolved.
- **Functional repair:** `App.tsx` `openEvidence(c)` now routes a direct DECISION→EVIDENCE open through existing `navigateNotebookPage('EVIDENCE')` before modifying Evidence owner or selected Quick Zone. If Decision saving/uncertain outcome or unsaved details require the current in-app warning, navigation returns false and the target Zone is not changed. If allowed, navigation clears the clean Decision editor, then opens the requested canonical Evidence Zone. No trade or fill is inferred/created and existing immutable decisions/evidence remain untouched.

- **Cross-stage regressions:** `quickDecisionSafety.test.ts` asserts Decision→Evidence requires leave gate for editor-open, never for unrelated pages/closed editors, and NO_TRADE/MISSED unsaved reasons remain protected. `requiredQuickStageCohort.test.ts` now exercises distinct persisted Candidate and Zone fields for two independently owned Zone IDs (same canonical candidate ID within each Zone), history snapshot label preservation, missing requirements only on Zone B, encoded owner focus, loaded-candidate membership and stage routing. This is unit/contract evidence, not installed UI/SQLite integration.

## PTJ-PACKAGE-A-CANONICAL-SESSION-STATE-POST-CONFIRM-2026-11-20
- **Scope/authorization:** Material source implementation and regression tests for cohesive Package A lifecycle/Quick Required navigation; 2026-11-20 Australia/Sydney. Only temporary PUBLIC feature branch `feature/ptj-package-a-cohesive-validation-repair-2026-11-18` and draft/unmerged PR #14. The user's unrelated accidental message is excluded from this project's scope. No Private repo commits/merges, user SQLite, migrations, binaries or installers.
- **Independently verified previous CI:** run `44553447499` for source SHA `b254d258f55063fb8a81f154c43e2f7a96258f5b` ended with both frontend and Rust SUCCESS. Previous direct clicked-Zone Evidence destination restoration is therefore CI-TESTED, not installed-Windows tested.
- **Concrete high-impact confirmed lifecycle gap:** `App.tsx` `confirm()` calls `localApi.confirmInitialPlan` then `refreshPlan()`. The latter refreshed `CurrentPlan`, timeline, consistency, trades and sidebar Sessions but did NOT reconcile `active:SessionSummary` with the persisted Session status. On successful confirmation the in-memory active Session could still be DRAFT despite an existing confirmed Plan. `quickValidationRoute(id,active?.status==='DRAFT',!!plan)` then mistakenly chose draft steps instead of the confirmed Quick PLAN for some required fields. Other status-sensitive UI could likewise treat the confirmed session as a draft until reopen. This is a real stage identity issue, not styling.
- **Implemented:** `app/src/sessionLifecycleSync.ts` provides pure `reconcileActiveSession` accepting only the exact requested canonical Session ID; it never fabricates an ACTIVE status, never replaces a different selected Session and does not change persisted data. `App.tsx` `refreshPlan()` captures the owner Session ID, checks `activeSessionIdRef` after awaited reads to prevent stale cross-session UI updates, and refreshes the canonical Session list + active Session status immediately after the persisted Plan read, BEFORE ancillary timeline/consistency/trade reads. Even if secondary reads fail, the confirmed Session's stage status can be rendered correctly.
- **Source commits:** `4222dd3d87e489bd0e090dab791489fd938b2756` helper; `5b61b15c56bff6d7542bfbba8952c2803ab83554` App lifecycle owner-safe reconciliation; `3bec1f3342c8789c0bda3ba2243437a431b189d2` helper regression suite; `d20521fa3b279d776923133798b8c0f64d816ca4` persisted status refresh prioritized ahead of ancillary reads; `3d4d7e82fb7aeb9fd410e55fccea1884e8fe9f79` Quick required navigation Draft→Plan transition regression.
- **Tests:** `sessionLifecycleSync.test.ts` verifies persisted DRAFT→ACTIVE→CLOSED transitions, exact canonical identity and instrument, no overwrite when a previously selected session becomes inactive while async work is pending, graceful missing-row behavior, and real `quickValidationRoute` stage change for a built-in Plan field and required custom Session field. Source tests were committed; no installed Windows runtime test or manual user acceptance has been performed.
- **Exact-head CI gate:** New code SHA `3d4d7e82fb7aeb9fd410e55fccea1884e8fe9f79`; GitHub Actions run `44565410995` was IN_PROGRESS at checkpoint time. CI-TESTED is PENDING until independently verified frontend and Rust success. Intermediate concurrent source pushes may have cancelled CI runs and do not demonstrate a product failure.
- **No-loss status:** All 12 Roadmap categories preserved; Package A #1–#5 still PARTIAL, full Field Manager eligible controls and custom typed history, multi-Zone integration flow, Group10 feedback Windows, and consolidated isolated DEMO remain open. Audited Data Correction and any schema migration still require separate authorization. Prior user Windows T01–T07 scoped PASS retained, not automatically extended to this change.
- **NEXT:** Independently verify run `44565410995`; if failure, correct only observed issue. Continue a cohesive actual-field/render/save/focus reconciliation for Session/Plan/Zone/Evidence/Decision, and wait on installer until the functional group can be accepted together. No release/merge/private writes without explicit approval.

## PTJ-PACKAGE-A-SESSION-OWNER-ASYNC-COHESIVE-GUARD-2026-11-20
- **Date/Package:** 2026-11-20 Australia/Sydney. Material cohesive Package A #1–#5 source implementation milestone (Session/Draft/Plan/Zone owner isolation), following user approval to finish categories 1–5 before one DEMO. PUBLIC feature branch `feature/ptj-package-a-cohesive-validation-repair-2026-11-18` only; PR #14 DRAFT/UNMERGED. Earlier stable installed DEMO, canonical PRIVATE repository and user SQLite untouched; no migration, installer, merge or asset upload.
- **Observed data-ownership defect:** `loadActive(s)` read persisted Draft/Plan/Timeline/Trade/Decision history through sequential awaits, then set shared React state without verifying that `s` still matched the user's currently selected Session. Rapid switching Session A→B while A's read finished late could replace B's Plan/Draft, intended instrument, Zone context and Decision history with A's values. Ancillary failure from an obsolete Session could also clear B's current Plan. Likewise old Draft-save/setup-save acknowledgements could advance B's draft step or replace B's active Session, and old required-custom-field verification could continue Plan confirmation after the user switched owners.
- **Implemented as one connected safety boundary:** introduce monotonic `sessionLoadRequestRef` and pure `isCurrentSessionLoad` in `app/src/sessionLifecycleSync.ts`; bump generation for active Session open, New Session and new Draft start. Check ownership after each Draft/Plan/Timeline/Consistency/Trade/Decision-history read, suppress obsolete success/error. Guard Draft-step and Draft Session setup-save acknowledgements, bind Confirm Plan's async required-field checks and canonical confirmation ID to initiating Session; prevent old validation from confirming a newly selected Session. Clear queued Decision→Evidence owner/target when crossing Session boundaries. Do not cancel a write already dispatched to its **original ID**; never misrepresent an uncertain write as failed or roll it back.
- **Source commits (in order):** `51c6fed9a50161b065f15c5d2d50532d891a55a0` load token on state reads; `fb7783473f9a6b1dc96e40678751b498cf5357a4` pure generation guard; `3e01472552d9287acc1b116168b095112ac8c87c` integrate helper; `defc8223f9103e8a9501370656f0831899cfd599` async Session tests; `402c620cda154acf26c94a316a44b82ed17ee3be` Draft/setup write-result guard; `cb4a504d512bb8d9daac678758e6132da26b7fba` save-result tests; `761d038994fdda0cd890f7c349354708e6b7b211` Plan confirm identity guard; `f34c4821a6148899ddf1d64dd71c991c198946c9` confirmation race tests; `4e4895b227bebd5eb83a7fc8b13e69354e6db5b2` clear queued Decision/Evidence target on owner switch. This is a single coherent source batch, **not nine accepted features**.
- **Regression evidence:** `sessionLifecycleSync.test.ts` verifies delayed previous-Draft read, confirmed Plan owner response, stale Decision-history generation, New Session invalidation, late Draft step/save setup acknowledgement and stale required-custom verification prior/post backend confirmation. Existing Stage/Zone Required and Decision/Evidence tests retained. GitHub Actions exact SOURCE SHA `4e4895b227bebd5eb83a7fc8b13e69354e6db5b2`, run `44648109923`; frontend SUCCESS independently observed, Rust IN_PROGRESS at time of checkpoint: **full CI PENDING**, not PASS until separately verified. Prior exact-source CI `44565410995` frontend+Rust SUCCESS remains baseline.
- **Acceptance/limits:** This gate addresses *async cross-Session ownership*. It does not prove all Field Manager controls rendered/persisted, full historical option behavior, or end-to-end multi-Zone UI in installed Windows. Package A categories #1–#5 remain PARTIAL; final combined Windows DEMO and user acceptance NOT YET. Original roadmap #6–#12 and all approved MR clauses preserved. Do not bypass separate Audited Data Correction migration authorization.
- **Next:** independently verify run `44648109923`; repair observed failures if needed. Continue actual eligible Field Manager bindings and custom owner/state persistence and then complete Plan→Zone→Evidence→TRADE/NO_TRADE/MISSED acceptance flow. Consolidate final exact-head CI; build ONE isolated Windows DEMO only after full functional package coverage, preserving previously verified T01–T07.
